    多层CrSBr中磁构型与切换的激子指纹#
    Excitonic fingerprints of magnetic configurations and switching in multilayer CrSBr#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Lukas Krelle, Ryan Tan, Jakob Conradi, Priyanka Mondal, Wenze Lan, Kseniia Mosina, Regine von Klitzing, Zdenek Sofer, Bernhard Urbaszek
    相关度
    9

    📄 摘要：电子态与磁性的耦合为磁信息的光学读取和控制提供了一条途径。在磁性半导体CrSBr中，激子与磁有序耦合，使其光学响应对底层磁化状态敏感。本文表明，体激子和表面激子的能量及振子强度可为磁构型和切换路径提供不同的光谱指纹。我们区分了两种磁化反转：一种是畴壁穿过光斑时表现为连续光谱演化的畴壁介导反转，另一种是突发的大面积磁化反转。利用所得到的激子指纹，我们重建了4层和5层CrSBr在从铁磁有序向反铁磁有序转变过程中依次出现的磁构型。我们进一步发现，对磁有序的灵敏度强烈依赖于激子：低能激子可分辨中间构型和与表面有关的构型，而高能激子则主要表现为铁磁与反铁磁共振之间的振子强度转移。这些结果确立了激子光谱学作为探测层状磁性半导体中层依赖磁构型及其切换路径的灵敏手段。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.24216v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The coupling between electronic states and magnetism provides a route towards optical readout and control of magnetic information. In the magnetic semiconductor CrSBr, excitons are coupled to magnetic order, making their optical response sensitive to the underlying magnetization. Here, we show that the energy and oscillator strength of bulk and surface excitons provide distinct spectroscopic fingerprints of magnetic configurations and switching pathways. We distinguish domain-wall-mediated magnetization reversal, manifested by continuous spectral evolution as a domain wall traverses the optical spot, from abrupt, large-area magnetization reversal. Using the resulting excitonic fingerprints, we reconstruct successive magnetic configurations in 4- and 5-layer CrSBr during the transition from ferromagnetic to antiferromagnetic order. We further find that the sensitivity to magnetic order is strongly exciton-dependent: low-energy excitons resolve intermediate and surface-related configurations, whereas a higher-energy exciton predominantly exhibits a transfer of oscillator strength between ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic resonances. These results establish excitonic spectroscopy as a sensitive probe of layer-dependent magnetic configurations and their switching pathways in layered magnetic semiconductors.

    💡 亮点：CrSBr中的激子与磁有序耦合，因此可用于光学读取磁信息。该工作表明，体激子与表面激子的能量和振子强度可提供不同磁构型及其切换途径的光谱指纹。利用这些指纹，作者重建了4层和5层CrSBr从铁磁序向反铁磁序转变时连续出现的磁构型。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入HfO₂ 铁电、二维滑移铁电、极化翻转、畴壁和疲劳/缺陷问题。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“把电场、极化、应变、氧空位和局域结构序参一起纳入轨迹数据；比较均匀翻转、局域成核和畴壁传播，并用 DFT/有效 Hamiltonian 对关键路径复核”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    利用分子动力学数据驱动的物理信息神经网络进行铁电体多尺度建模：参数识别与场重建#
    Multiscale Modelling of Ferroelectrics using a Physics-Informed Neural Network Driven by Molecular Dynamics Data: Parameter Identification and Field Reconstruction#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Xuejian Wang, Frank Wendler, Hikaru Auzuma, Michael Zaiser, Shuji Ogata, Ryo Kobayashi, Lei Zeng, Xingchen Tan
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：在铁电体多尺度建模中，将原子尺度模拟与连续尺度相场模型（PFM）结合仍是一项基础性挑战。一个关键困难在于，如何在连续建模框架中忠实捕捉离散的原子尺度信息，同时准确表征介观尺度的材料行为。本文使用由分子动力学（MD）数据驱动的物理信息神经网络（PINN）。该网络的损失函数由两部分构成：一部分是监督项，用于拟合由含畴壁体系的 MD 模拟得到的离散空间极化分布；另一部分是基于物理的项，其中纳入稳态 PFM 偏微分方程（PDE）的残差。为确保不同损失分量之间稳定且平衡的训练，我们采用自适应梯度归一化方法（GradNorm）动态调整任务权重。通过最小化总损失，该模型不仅能够重建连续尺度下的极化场及其相关应变、应力和能量图景，还能够识别相场模型的关键物理参数，包括特征能量密度、特征长度因子、梯度能各向异性因子以及朗道多项式系数。通过在 COMSOL Multiphysics 中使用 PINN 预测的物理参数，并在有限元框架内求解相应 PDE，我们证明这些参数能够准确再现铁电畴结构及相关材料响应，包括应力/应变分布和能量图景。该框架为建立原子尺度与连续尺度描述之间的多尺度联系提供了一种有效方法，并有望针对广泛材料体系直接从极化分布中推断潜在物理性质。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.24733v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: In multiscale modeling of ferroelectrics, combining atomistic simulation with continuum-scale phase-field models (PFM) remains a fundamental challenge. A key difficulty lies in faithfully capturing discrete atomic-level information within a continuum modeling framework, while accurately representing material behavior at the mesoscale. In this paper, a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) driven by molecular dynamics (MD) data is used. The loss function of the network consists of a supervised term that fits the discrete spatial polarization distributions obtained from MD simulations of systems containing domain walls, and a physics-based term that incorporates the residuals of partial differential equations (PDEs) of steady-state PFM. To ensure stable and balanced training among the different loss components, adaptive gradient normalization (GradNorm) is used to dynamically adjust the task weights. By minimizing the total loss, the model not only reconstructs the polarization field along with the associated strain, stress, and energy landscape at the continuum scale, but also identifies critical physical parameters of the phase-field model, including the characteristic energy density, characteristic length factor, gradient energy anisotropy factor, and Landau polynomial coefficients. By using the PINN-predicted physical parameters in COMSOL Multiphysics to solve the corresponding PDEs within a finite element framework, we demonstrate that these parameters enable accurate reproduction of the ferroelectric domain structure and the associated material response, including stress/strain distributions and energy landscape. This framework provides an effective methodology for establishing multiscale connections between atomistic and continuum descriptions, and holds the potential to infer underlying physical properties directly from polarization distributions for a wide range of materials.

    💡 亮点：本文提出以分子动力学数据驱动的物理信息神经网络，用于连接铁电体的原子尺度模拟与连续相场模型。网络同时拟合含畴壁体系的离散极化分布，并纳入稳态相场方程残差；采用 GradNorm 自适应平衡各损失项。该方法可重建连续尺度的极化、应变、应力和能量图景，并识别相场模型关键参数，且经 COMSOL 有限元计算验证。

    将结构光量子编码为面内拓扑自旋纹理#
    Quantum encoding of structured light into in-plane topological spin textures#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Pavel A. Vorobyev, Daichi Kurebayashi, Oleg A. Tretiakov
    相关度
    8

    📄 摘要：结构光提供了一种利用多个可调光学自由度控制光与物质相互作用的有力手段。我们使用微磁学模拟，研究了在分别具有 Cnv 和 D2d 对称性的手性铁磁薄膜中，通过脉冲拉盖尔—高斯光学涡旋使非对称双磁子和反双磁子成核的过程。对于轨道角动量（OAM）绝对值 |m|=1 的光学涡旋，圆偏振光束通过自旋角动量、OAM 和磁手性的共同作用，以确定性的方式成核出单个双磁子或反双磁子；而线偏振光束会产生其拓扑荷直接遵循 OAM 的纹理（Q=m）。具有 |m|>1 的光学涡旋会成核出由多个自旋纹理组成的团簇及其他构型，其形态和拓扑荷对光学量子数与脉冲参数表现出敏感依赖。这些发现揭示了一条拓扑选择性写入路径，即将光学量子数编码到不同的面内拓扑磁态中。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.23856v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Structured light offers a powerful means of controlling light-matter interactions through multiple tunable optical degrees of freedom. Using micromagnetic simulations, we investigate the nucleation of asymmetric bimerons and antibimerons by pulsed Laguerre-Gaussian optical vortices in chiral ferromagnetic thin films with Cₙᵥ and D₂d symmetries, respectively. For optical vortices with orbital angular momentum (OAM) |m|=1, circularly polarized beams deterministically nucleate a single bimeron or antibimeron via the interplay of spin angular momentum, OAM, and magnetic chirality, whereas linearly polarized beams produce textures whose topological charge directly follows the OAM (Q=m). Optical vortices with OAM |m|>1 nucleate clusters and other configurations composed of multiple spin textures, whose morphology and topological charge depend sensitively on the optical quantum numbers and pulse parameters. These findings reveal a route to topology-selective writing through the encoding of optical quantum numbers into distinct in-plane topological magnetic states.

    💡 亮点：结构光具有多个可调光学自由度，为控制光与物质相互作用提供了途径。利用微磁学模拟，研究考察了具有 Cnv 和 D2d 对称性的手性铁磁薄膜中，脉冲拉盖尔—高斯光学涡旋诱导非对称双磁子和反双磁子的成核。对于轨道角动量绝对值为 1 的涡旋，圆偏振光可确定性地产生单个纹理，线偏振光则产生满足 Q=m 的纹理；更高轨道角动量会产生由多个纹理组成的团簇。结果展示了将光学量子数编码到不同面内拓扑磁态中的选择性写入方案。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    中子散射证实α-Cu2P2O7中二维耦合的自旋二聚化反铁磁晶格#
    Neutron scattering evidence for two-dimensionally coupled spin-dimerized antiferromagnetic lattice in α-Cu2P2O7#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 B. Ghanta, K. S. Chikara, M. Ghanathe, L. Keller, D. Voneshen, A. K. Bera
    相关度
    7

    📄 摘要：低维量子磁体α-Cu2P2O7的微观磁模型一直存在争议。我们利用温度依赖的非弹性中子散射、中子衍射、磁化测量以及全面的自旋波建模，对其磁基态和激发谱进行了综合研究。我们的结果明确确立了α-Cu2P2O7为bc面内二维耦合的自旋二聚化反铁磁晶格，其中主导的反铁磁交换J2=7.73 meV，以下称为“二聚体内交换”；二维晶格中还存在较弱的交换耦合J1、J3和J4，以下称为“二聚体间交换”。这一结论与基于LDA的密度泛函理论一致，但与此前GGA+U的预测相反。我们发现，主导的二聚体内反铁磁交换发生在第七近邻Cu-Cu离子对之间[d(Cu-Cu)=5.125(3) Å]，而非结构二聚体中最近邻的Cu-Cu离子对之间[d(Cu-Cu)=3.014(1) Å]。弱层间耦合(J5=0.03 meV)使系统在TN=25 K以下形成长程反铁磁有序。我们进一步识别出一种与畸变CuO5多面体有关的弱单离子各向异性，它在自旋激发谱中打开能隙，并驱动磁场诱导的变磁相变。系统的自旋波计算阐明了层间耦合J5和各向异性项D在不同反铁磁布里渊区中心产生两个不同能隙时所发挥的不同作用。作为外加磁场函数的互补中子衍射和磁化测量发现了此前被忽略的、位于约13 kOe处的变磁相变，并得以构建H-T平面内的磁相图。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.24141v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The microscopic magnetic model of the low-dimensional quantum magnet alpha-Cu2P2O7 has remained controversial. We present a comprehensive study of its magnetic ground state and excitation spectrum using temperature-dependent inelastic neutron scattering, neutron diffraction, magnetization measurements, and comprehensive spin-wave modeling. Our results unambiguously establish alpha-Cu2P2O7 as a two-dimensionally coupled spin-dimerized antiferromagnetic (AF) lattice within the bc plane, with a dominant AF exchange J2 = 7.73 meV (hereafter referred to as "intradimer exchange") and weaker exchange couplings J1, J3, and J4 in the two-dimensional lattice (hereafter referred to as "interdimer exchange"), in agreement with LDA-based density functional theory and in contrast to previous GGA+U predictions. The dominant intradimer AF exchange is found between seventh-nearest-neighbor Cu-Cu ion pairs [d(Cu-Cu) = 5.125(3) A] rather than nearest-neighbor Cu-Cu ion pairs [d(Cu-Cu) = 3.014(1) A] of the structural dimers. Weak interlayer coupling (J5 = 0.03 meV) stabilizes long-range antiferromagnetic order below TN = 25 K. We further identify a weak single-ion anisotropy, associated with the distorted CuO5 polyhedra, that opens a gap in the spin-excitation spectrum and drives a field-induced metamagnetic transition. Systematic spin-wave calculations elucidate the distinct roles of interlayer coupling J5 and anisotropy term D in producing two distinct energy gaps at different antiferromagnetic zone centers. Complementary neutron diffraction and magnetization measurements as a function of applied magnetic field uncover a previously overlooked metamagnetic transition near 13 kOe and allow construction of the magnetic phase diagram in the H-T plane.

    💡 亮点：该工作结合非弹性中子散射、衍射、磁化测量和自旋波建模，确定α-Cu2P2O7为bc面内二维耦合的自旋二聚化反铁磁体。主导的二聚体内反铁磁交换为J2=7.73 meV，发生于第七近邻Cu-Cu对而非结构二聚体中的最近邻对。弱层间耦合和单离子各向异性分别稳定长程有序并导致激发能隙及磁场诱导的变磁相变。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入热力学知情 ML、机器学习势、自由能采样、CALPHAD/团簇展开。从摘要能确认的线索包括：密度泛函理论和电子结构计算，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“在 DFT 能量/力/应力之外加入跨温度构型、相对自由能、熵或热膨胀信息；用主动学习补采相变、畴壁和相竞争区域，再用热力学积分、MC 或有限温度 MD 验证”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    Cu-Ru 基双钙钛矿 La₂₋ₓSmₓCuRuO₆（x = 0、1、2）氧化物中的可调磁挫折#
    Tunable Magnetic Frustration in the Cu-Ru-based Double Perovskite La₂₋ₓSmₓCuRuO₆ (x = 0, 1, 2) Oxides#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Soumya Ghorai, Samir Rom, Irina Shamova, N. K. Karn, Tamanna Kumari, A. K. Shukla, Sanjoy Kr. Mahatha, O. Volkova, Nitesh Kumar Tanusri Saha Dasgupta, Setti Thirupathaiah
    相关度
    7

    📄 摘要：本研究考察了通过固相反应法合成的铜—钌基氧化物双钙钛矿 La₂₋ₓSmₓCuRuO₆（x = 0、1、2）的结构、磁性和电子性质。X 射线衍射分析表明，所有化合物均结晶为单斜对称结构；从 La³⁺ 变为较小尺寸的阳离子 Sm³⁺ 时，结构畸变程度增大。电阻率研究显示，所有化合物均表现出绝缘行为；由于存在反位无序，低温下以可变范围跳跃输运为主。交流磁化率和热容测量表明，含 Sm 化合物中的磁挫折受到抑制，并由此影响其磁性行为。我们的第一性原理计算表明，晶格畸变与 Sm 磁性的共同作用在减弱磁挫折方面发挥关键作用，从而解释了实验观察。这些发现阐明了 Cu-Ru 双钙钛矿中晶体结构与磁性之间的复杂相互作用，并为通过稀土离子取代调控磁性开辟了途径。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.23741v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: In this study, we investigate structural, magnetic, and electronic properties of the copper-ruthenate based oxide double perovskite La₂₋ₓSmₓCuRuO₆ (x = 0, 1, 2), synthesized through the solid-state reaction method. X-ray diffraction analysis reveals that all compounds crystallize in a monoclinic symmetry, with varying degree of structural distortion that increases in moving from La³⁺ to smaller size cation Sm³⁺. Electrical resistivity studies indicate insulating behaviour in all compounds, with variable-range-hopping domination at low temperatures, due to presence of anti-site disorder. AC susceptibility and heat capacity measurements suggest suppression of frustration in Sm-bearing compounds, affecting the magnetic behavior. Our first-principles calculations suggest that the combined effects of lattice distortion and Sm magnetism play a crucial role in weakening magnetic frustration, thereby rationalizing the experimental observations. These findings shed light on the complex interplay of crystal structure and magnetism in Cu-Ru double perovskites, and open up an avenue for tuning of magnetic properties through rare-earth-ion substitution.

    💡 亮点：本研究考察了固相反应法制备的 La₂₋ₓSmₓCuRuO₆（x = 0、1、2）的结构、磁性和电子性质。所有化合物均为单斜晶系，随着较小的 Sm³⁺ 取代 La³⁺，结构畸变增强；它们均表现为绝缘体，低温下由可变范围跳跃输运主导。交流磁化率和热容测量表明，含 Sm 化合物中的磁挫折受到抑制，第一性原理计算则指出晶格畸变与 Sm 磁性共同削弱了磁挫折，说明稀土离子取代能够调控磁性。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：第一性原理标注与结构优化，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    Simulations reveal asymmetric diffusion of magnetic skyrmions through an off-center gate#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 Physics News🔥 AI 6

    📄 摘要：扩散是一种基本的自然现象，可以在广泛的长度和时间尺度上观察到。它在许多领域中发挥关键作用，包括物理学、生物学和经济学。特别是，粒子系统的非对称扩散或定向扩散正受到越来越多的关注，其实际应用包括开发新型人工智能（AI）硬件；在这类硬件中，这种扩散有望实现非线性、由几何结构控制的信息处理。

    Diffusion is a fundamental natural phenomenon that can be observed across a wide range of length and time scales. It plays a key role in many fields, including physics, biology and economics. In particular, asymmetric or directional diffusion of particle systems has attracted growing interest for practical applications, including the development of unconventional artificial intelligence (AI) hardware, where it could enable nonlinear, geometry-controlled information processing.

    可由电场和磁场调控的翘曲二维材料中的间谷磁三元子#
    Intervalley Magnetotrions Tunable by Electric and Magnetic Fields in Buckled Two-Dimensional Materials#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Roman Ya. Kezerashvili, Shalva M. Tsiklauri, Anastasia Spiridonova
    相关度
    5

    📄 摘要：我们建立了翘曲二维材料（包括硅烯、锗烯和锡烯）中间谷磁三元子的理论框架，并研究了这些体系在垂直电场和磁场作用下的性质。在有效质量近似下，我们利用 Rytova--Keldysh 相互作用势建立了三粒子薛定谔方程，并在高磁场区域对其进行分析。我们证明，当电子与空穴的有效质量相等时，间谷三元子构成一个特殊情形，此时质心运动与内部运动能够严格分离。质心运动由二维谐振子 Hamiltonian 控制，从而产生量子化的朗道态；其能量形成可由电场调控的朗道曲面，并受磁场以及电场对载流子有效质量的影响所控制。通过在超球谐方法框架内求解三体薛定谔方程，我们研究了内部运动。数值计算表明，三元子结合能随磁场和电场均单调增加，这归因于磁约束效应与电场诱导的有效质量增强效应的共同作用。最强的结合出现在硅烯中，其次是锡烯和锗烯。本工作统一描述了 Xene 单层中的集体质心运动和内部动力学，并表明这两个自由度可以分别通过外部电场和磁场进行操控。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.24581v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We develop a theoretical framework for intervalley magnetotrions in buckled two-dimensional materials, including silicene, germanene, and stanene, subjected to perpendicular electric and magnetic fields. Within the effective-mass approximation, the three-particle Schrödinger equation is formulated with the Rytova--Keldysh interaction potential and analyzed in the high-magnetic-field regime. We demonstrate that intervalley trions with equal electron and hole effective masses constitute an exceptional case for which the center-of-mass and internal motions separate exactly. The center-of-mass motion is governed by a two-dimensional harmonic-oscillator Hamiltonian, leading to quantized Landau states whose energies form electrically tunable Landau surfaces controlled by the magnetic field and the electric-field dependence of the carrier effective masses. The internal motion is investigated by solving the three-body Schrödinger equation within the framework of the hyperspherical harmonics method. Numerical calculations reveal that the trion binding energy increases monotonically with both magnetic and electric fields owing to the combined effects of magnetic confinement and electric-field-induced enhancement of the effective masses. The strongest binding is obtained for silicene, followed by stanene and germanene. The present work provides a unified description of both the collective center-of-mass motion and the internal dynamics of magnetotrions in Xene monolayers, demonstrating that both degrees of freedom can be independently manipulated by external electric and magnetic fields.

    💡 亮点：本文建立了翘曲二维材料（包括硅烯、锗烯和锡烯）中间谷磁三元子的理论框架，并研究其在垂直电场和磁场下的性质。在有效质量近似和高磁场条件下，当电子与空穴有效质量相等时，质心运动与内部运动可以严格分离。数值结果表明，磁三元子结合能随磁场和电场单调增加，其中硅烯中的结合最强，其次为锡烯和锗烯；质心运动与内部动力学可由外部电磁场独立调控。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：有效哈密顿量或机器学习哈密顿量，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”补充训练/验证集，再比较《可由电场和磁场调控的翘曲二维材料中的间谷磁三元子》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

    近邻效应诱导超导体中自发涡旋的成像与表征#
    Imaging and characterization of spontaneous vortices in a proximity-induced superconductor#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Iku Nakaaki, Kotaro Taki, Mio Nomura, Haruna Ishimaru, Minagi Yono, Jun Chen, Hiroyo Segawa, Akiko Nakamura, Taku Moronaga, Minoru Tachiki, Shuuichi Ooi, Shunichi Arisawa, Tsutomu Nojima, Takashi Uchino
    相关度
    4

    📄 摘要：观测自发对称性破缺对于理解从无序态到有序态的连续二级相变至关重要，这一过程往往会导致拓扑缺陷的形成。在超导体中，这类拓扑缺陷表现为量子化涡旋。然而，在均匀超导体中，自发涡旋的形成和观测具有挑战性，因为通常需要极快的冷却速率（>10⁸ K/s）才能实现这一目的。在此，我们对一种MgB2基近邻效应诱导超导体进行了扫描超导量子干涉器件显微镜（SSM）测量，该体系本征上是不均匀的。在该体系中，冷却期间各个超导畴会独立达到内部平衡，并在通过长程近邻耦合建立全局相干之前各自选择其相位。SSM测量表明，即使在相对较慢的冷却速率（约0.2 K/s）下，涡旋也会自发成核。我们还发现，在近零磁场条件下，具有不同极性、尺寸和形状的涡旋以随机方式出现。自发涡旋的几何尺度比磁场诱导的Abrikosov涡旋更为延展。基于伦敦模型的磁场剖面分析阐明，这些延展涡旋的穿透深度异常大，超过数微米。自发涡旋这种不同寻常的形态，极有可能记录了涡旋形成瞬间被冻结的信息。我们的发现不仅为理解该近邻效应诱导超导体系在相变早期存在的局域相位差提供了见解，也为高度无序和非均匀超导体系中拓扑缺陷的结构、形成与稳定化提供了认识。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2607.14697v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Observation of spontaneous symmetry breaking is crucial for understanding continuous second-order phase transitions from disordered to ordered states, which often leads to the formation of topological defects. In superconductors, such topological defects manifest as quantized vortices. However, the formation and observation of spontaneous vortices in a uniform superconductor are challenging because extremely rapid cooling (>108 K/s) is generally required for that purpose. Here we conducted scanning superconducting quantum interference device microscope (SSM) measurements on an MgB2-based proximity-induced superconductor, an intrinsically inhomogeneous system. In this system, individual superconducting domains will reach internal equilibrium independently during cooling and choose their own phase before the global phase coherence is established via the long-range proximity coupling. The SSM measurements demonstrate that vortices are nucleated spontaneously even at a relatively slow cooling rate (~0.2 K/s). We also find that the vortices with different polarities, sizes, and shapes appear stochastically under near-zero-field conditions. The geometry of the spontaneous vortices is more extended than that of the field-induced Abrikosov vortices. Magnetic field profile analysis based on the London model elucidates that penetration depths of the extended vortices are anomalously large, exceeding several micrometers. This unusual morphology of the spontaneous vortices most likely imprints the information that is frozen at the moment of vortex formation. Our findings not only provide insights into the local phase differences present in the early stage of the phase transition in this proximity-induced superconducting system, but they also shed insights into the structure, formation, and stabilization of topological defects in highly disordered and inhomogeneous superconducting systems.

    💡 亮点：自发对称性破缺常伴随拓扑缺陷形成，但均匀超导体中自发涡旋通常需要极快速冷却才可观测。该研究利用扫描超导量子干涉器件显微镜，对本征非均匀的MgB2基近邻效应诱导超导体进行测量，在约0.2 K/s的缓慢冷却下观察到随机成核的自发涡旋。其尺寸和形态比外场诱导的Abrikosov涡旋更为延展，伦敦模型分析表明其穿透深度异常大，可超过数微米。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 缺陷、畴壁、成核和开关动力学对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    微流控通道中铁磁向列液晶的解景观#
    Solution landscapes of ferronematics in microfluidic channels#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 James Dalby, Apala Majumdar
    相关度
    3

    📄 摘要：我们研究了一维通道几何中铁磁向列液晶的解景观，即磁性纳米颗粒在向列液晶主体中的稀悬浮体系。采用Bisht等人（2020年）提出的建模框架，允许的铁磁向列构型被表示为适当定义的铁磁向列能量的临界点。临界点有两类：充分利用所有自由度的完整临界点，以及序重构（OR）临界点。序重构临界点具有多畴结构，各个多畴之间由畴壁分隔。我们发现，铁磁向列体系通常是多稳态的，即其自由能具有多个彼此竞争的稳定临界点，这些临界点对应于在物理上可能被观测到的构型；这种多稳态性并非先验显而易见。此外，我们还发现了多个稳定和不稳定的序重构解，它们在畴壁的位置和数量上有所不同。在平衡过程和非平衡过程中，畴壁分别可以充当结合位点和引导输运的轨道。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2309.16841v3 Announce Type: replace Abstract: We study solution landscapes for ferronematics, i.e., a dilute suspension of magnetic nanoparticles in a nematic liquid crystal host, in a one-dimensional (1D) channel geometry. Adopting the modelling framework in Bisht et al.(2020), the admissible ferronematic configurations are modelled in terms of critical points of an appropriately defined ferronematic energy. There are two types of critical points: full critical points that exploit all degrees of freedom and order reconstruction (OR) critical points. OR critical points have polydomain structures, with the polydomains separated by domain walls. We find that ferronematic systems are typically multistable, i.e., the free energy has multiple competing stable critical points, which model potentially physically observable configurations; the multistability is not a priori obvious. Further, we discover multiple stable and unstable OR solutions that differ in the locations and multiplicity of domain walls. The domain walls can act as binding sites in equilibrium processes and rafts for guided transport phenomena in non-equilibrium processes.

    💡 亮点：本文研究一维通道几何中铁磁向列液晶的解景观，即磁性纳米颗粒在向列液晶主体中的稀悬浮体系。模型将允许的铁磁向列构型表示为相应能量的临界点，其中包括充分利用全部自由度的完整临界点和具有序重构结构的临界点。研究发现，该体系通常具有多稳态，并存在多个稳定或不稳定的序重构解，其差异体现在畴壁的位置和数量上。畴壁可在平衡过程中充当结合位点，并在非平衡过程中作为引导输运的轨道。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入热力学知情 ML、机器学习势、自由能采样、CALPHAD/团簇展开。从摘要能确认的线索包括：自由能面和热力学积分/采样，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“在 DFT 能量/力/应力之外加入跨温度构型、相对自由能、熵或热膨胀信息；用主动学习补采相变、畴壁和相竞争区域，再用热力学积分、MC 或有限温度 MD 验证”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：热力学知情 ML、机器学习势、自由能采样、CALPHAD/团簇展开已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到HfO₂ 基铁电、二维滑移铁电、钙钛矿和磁性氧化物。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 缺陷、畴壁、成核和开关动力学在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    文献研究：QML for Quantum Sensing under Measurement-Induced Information Loss#
    QML for Quantum Sensing under Measurement-Induced Information Loss#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / cs.AI👤 Sounak Bhowmik, Himanshu Thapliyal
    相关度
    2
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.23934v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: Nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond can serve as highly sensitive solid-state quantum sensors for high-sensitivity magnetometry. However, in the noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) era, extracting reliable information from noisy, finite-shot, and measurement-limited sensing data remains a considerable challenge. Whereas, quantum machine learning (QML) offers a potential path to improve parameter estimation by learning nonlinear relationships between quantum-sensing data and the underlying physical signal. In this work, we investigate the role of QML in magnetic-field estimation within an NV center-inspired magnetometry setting. We formulated magnetic field sensing as a supervised regression task. We compared the performance of several classical machine learning models trained on measurement-based classical data with that of quantum kernel-based models trained on pre-measurement coherent quantum states. Our objective is to isolate the impact of measurement-induced information loss and therefore provide a theoretical upper bound on the sensing performance. The upper bound is achievable only when coherent quantum information is directly available to the learning model. Our results show that QML-based sensing performance improves significantly with coherent quantum-state information, and not much with changes in model complexity or learning paradigm. This observation underscores the importance of learning pipelines that tightly integrate quantum sensors and QML models to enhance magnetic field sensing under realistic constraints.

    Kitaev-Heisenberg-Γ模型的相图：经典与量子磁性、受挫和次要相互作用#
    Phase diagram of the Kitaev-Heisenberg-Γ model: Classical and quantum magnetism, frustration, and subdominant interactions#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Kiyu Fukui, Yukitoshi Motome
    相关度
    7.5

    📄 摘要：Kitaev自旋液体为严格可解量子自旋液体态提供了一个罕见范例。过去二十年的密集研究已经识别出多种可能的候选材料。然而，在真实材料中，Kitaev相互作用不可避免地伴随着诸如Heisenberg相互作用和Γ相互作用等额外磁相互作用。这些相互作用常常在低温下诱发磁有序，因此在寻找和设计Kitaev自旋液体候选材料时，澄清它们的影响至关重要。本研究采用最先进的数值技术，从经典和量子两个角度重新考察Kitaev-Heisenberg-Γ模型的基态相图。在经典情形中，我们揭示出多种非共线有序，其中出现了各种带有或不带有非公度调制的非共线多Q磁有序。在量子情形中，我们阐明量子涨落会抑制经典情形中发现的许多竞争有序，从而导致主导的非公度有序数量减少。我们还识别出高度受挫的区域，在这些区域中，螺旋自旋液体态以及新的磁有序态可能由其他额外磁相互作用稳定。我们的结果为经典和量子自旋的Kitaev-Heisenberg-Γ模型提供了全面视角，并为解释候选材料的实验结果以及寻找和设计实现Kitaev自旋液体的新材料提供了有价值的指引。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2606.13263v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: The Kitaev spin liquid provides a rare example of exactly solvable quantum spin liquid states. Intensive research over the past two decades has identified a variety of its candidate materials. In real materials, however, the Kitaev interaction is inevitably accompanied by additional magnetic interactions such as the Heisenberg and Γ interactions. These interactions often induce magnetic ordering at low temperatures, making it essential to clarify their effects in the search for and design of Kitaev spin liquid candidate materials. In this study, we revisit the ground-state phase diagram of the Kitaev-Heisenberg-Γ model from both classical and quantum perspectives, using state-of-the-art numerical techniques. In the classical case, we reveal a zoo of noncollinear orders, where a variety of noncollinear multiple-Q magnetic orders with and without incommensurate modulations emerge. In the quantum case, we unravel that quantum fluctuations suppress many of the competing orders found in the classical case, resulting in a reduced number of dominant incommensurate orders. We further identify highly frustrated regions, where spiral spin liquid states as well as new magnetically ordered states are potentially stabilized by other additional magnetic interactions. Our results provide a comprehensive perspective on the Kitaev-Heisenberg-Γ model for both classical and quantum spins and offer a valuable guide not only for interpreting experimental results on candidate materials, but also for searching and designing new materials to realize the Kitaev spin liquid.

    💡 亮点：Kitaev自旋液体是少见的可严格求解量子自旋液体，而实际材料中的Kitaev相互作用通常伴随Heisenberg和Γ相互作用。本文利用先进数值方法，从经典和量子两个角度重新研究Kitaev-Heisenberg-Γ模型的基态相图。经典情形呈现多种非共线多Q磁序，量子涨落则压制其中许多竞争序并减少主导的非公度有序；研究还识别出可能由额外相互作用稳定螺旋自旋液体或新磁序的高度受挫区域。

    WSe₂—NiPS₃ 磁性范德华异质结构中的光谱可编程自旋极化光电流#
    Spectrally Programmable Spin-Polarized Photocurrents in WSe₂₋NiPS₃ Magnetic van der Waals Heterostructures#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Rajesh Kumar Yadav, Michal Poplinger, Adi Levi, Adi Harchol, Nirman Chakraborty, Thomas Brumme, Thomas Heine, Efrat Lifshitz, Doron Naveh
    相关度
    7.5

    📄 摘要：在半导体中高效产生和控制自旋极化电流仍是自旋电子学面临的核心挑战，尤其受到阻抗失配以及依赖磁场或铁磁接触的限制。在此，我们提出一种用于光谱可编程自旋输运的材料平台，该平台基于将反铁磁半导体 NiPS₃ 与 WSe₂ 结合起来的范德华异质结构。在 p-n 二极管架构中，圆偏振激发产生显著的光电导共振；在 Néel 温度附近，自旋极化率可达到 80%，在室温下仍保持 30%。值得注意的是，选定的光谱带在磁相变前后保持其极化符号，证明了稳健且受光谱保护的自旋极化电流产生。偏振分辨的光生伏打测量揭示，主导机制是圆偏振注入电流机制，从而证实了自旋极化载流子输运。第一性原理计算表明，外加电场会诱导界面杂化和自旋—层锁定，导致局域对称性破缺和增强的光吸收，同时保持整体时间反演对称性。这些结果确立了将激发光谱调谐作为自旋输运的新控制手段，使得无需磁场或偏振切换即可产生自旋电流。我们的发现表明，磁性范德华异质结构是实现光自旋电子学功能和光谱可编程自旋电子器件的多用途平台。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.23832v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Efficient generation and control of spin-polarized currents in semiconductors remain central challenges for spin-based electronics, particularly due to impedance mismatch and the reliance on magnetic fields or ferromagnetic contacts. Here, we introduce a materials platform for spectrally programmable spin transport based on a van der Waals heterostructure combining the antiferromagnetic semiconductor NiPS₃ with WSe₂. In a p-n diode architecture, circularly polarized excitation produces pronounced photoconductive resonances with spin polarization reaching 80% near the Neel temperature and persisting at 30% at room temperature. Remarkably, selected spectral bands retain their polarization sign across the magnetic phase transition, evidencing robust, spectrally protected spin-polarized current generation. Polarization-resolved photogalvanic measurements reveal a dominant circular injection-current mechanism, confirming spin-polarized carrier transport. First-principles calculations show that an applied electric field induces interfacial hybridization and spin-layer locking, giving rise to localized symmetry breaking and enhanced optical absorption while preserving global time-reversal symmetry. These results establish spectral tuning of excitation as a new control knob for spin transport, enabling spin-current generation without magnetic fields or polarization switching. Our findings position magnetic van der Waals heterostructures as a versatile platform for opto-spintronic functionality and spectrally programmable spintronic devices.

    💡 亮点：本文提出一种基于反铁磁半导体 NiPS₃ 与 WSe₂ 组成的范德华异质结构的光谱可编程自旋输运平台。在 p-n 二极管结构中，圆偏振激发产生显著光电导共振，在 Néel 温度附近自旋极化率达到 80%，室温仍为 30%；部分光谱带在磁相变两侧保持极化符号，显示出受光谱保护的自旋极化电流。计算表明，电场诱导的界面杂化和自旋—层锁定增强了局域对称性破缺与光吸收，同时保持整体时间反演对称性。

    交替磁结中的自旋极化安德烈夫分子与反常非局域约瑟夫森效应#
    Spin-polarized Andreev molecules and anomalous nonlocal Josephson effects in altermagnetic junctions#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Sayan Mondal, Jorge Cayao
    相关度
    7.5

    📄 摘要：交替磁性已成为实现非平凡约瑟夫森相位的一种有前景的要素，但迄今为止主要是在单个约瑟夫森结中得到研究。本文考虑了两个具有自旋单态s波超导性的约瑟夫森结之间的相干耦合，并证明当耦合较弱时，d波交替磁性会由于各结安德烈夫束缚态的杂化而产生自旋极化的安德烈夫分子。有趣的是，这些自旋极化安德烈夫分子会诱导一种反常非局域约瑟夫森效应：一个约瑟夫森结中的电流由另一个结两端的相位变化所驱动，并会出现源于交替磁性的0-π转变和φ₀转变。此外，由自旋极化安德烈夫分子承载的非局域约瑟夫森电流表现出非互易临界电流，从而实现一种非局域约瑟夫森二极管效应，其极性可由交替磁强度和右侧相位调节。我们的发现表明，交替磁性是设计非局域自旋约瑟夫森现象的一个有前景的平台。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2603.04954v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Altermagnetism has emerged as a promising ingredient for realizing nontrivial Josephson phases, but so far explored in single Josephson junctions. In this work, we consider the coherent coupling of two Josephson junctions with spin-singlet s-wave superconductivity and demonstrate that d-wave altermagnetism gives rise to spin-polarized Andreev molecules due to the hybridization of Andreev bound states of each junction when the coupling is weak. Interestingly, these spin-polarized Andreev molecules induce an anomalous nonlocal Josephson effect, where the current flow across one Josephson junction due to phase changes across the other junction develops 0-π and φ₀ transitions originating from altermagnetism. Furthermore, the nonlocal Josephson current carried by spin-polarized Andreev molecules exhibits nonreciprocal critical currents, enabling a nonlocal Josephson diode effect whose polarity is tunable by the altermagnetic strength and right phase. Our findings put forward altermagnetism as a promising arena for designing nonlocal spin Josephson phenomena.

    💡 亮点：交替磁性被视为实现非平凡约瑟夫森相位的有前景要素，但此前主要研究单个约瑟夫森结。本文研究两个自旋单态s波超导约瑟夫森结的相干耦合，发现d波交替磁性可在弱耦合下通过安德烈夫束缚态杂化形成自旋极化安德烈夫分子。这些分子诱导反常非局域约瑟夫森效应，并可产生极性可调的非局域约瑟夫森二极管效应。

    准一维反铁磁体 BaCo₂V₂O₈ 中的反常磁热效应#
    Anomalous magnetocaloric effects in the quasi-one-dimensional antiferromagnet BaCo₂V₂O₈#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Jiahao Yang, Chao Dong, Xinlong Shi, Zhuo Wang, Tiantian Li, Liusuo Wu, Junfeng Wang, Zhangzhen He, Liang Li, Yongkang Luo, Jianda Wu
    相关度
    7.5

    📄 摘要：我们研究了准一维类 Ising 反铁磁体 BaCo₂V₂O₈ 在横向磁场下的热力学性质；其倾斜的螺旋链几何结构和各向异性的朗德 g 张量产生了空间调制的 Zeeman 耦合。角度分辨的磁热效应（MCE）测量表明，当 H 平行于 [110] 时，在横向场 Ising 临界场附近存在一个高场温度极小值；当旋转磁场方向时，该极小值仍然存在，并且仅发生微弱移动。张量网络计算显示，旋转诱导的交错横向场会迅速降低 Ising 临界场，并且磁 Grüneisen 比在高场温度极小值附近发生变号，这与实验结果相符。我们的结果表明，即使远离 Ising 临界区域，主导性的 MCE 响应仍可持续存在，这为通过调控量子磁体中的各向异性 Zeeman 耦合构型实现磁制冷提供了一条途径。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.24463v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We investigate the transverse-field thermodynamics of the quasi-one-dimensional Ising-like antiferromagnet BaCo₂V₂O₈, whose tilted screw-chain geometry and anisotropic Landé g tensor generate spatially modulated Zeeman couplings. Angle-resolved magnetocaloric-effect (MCE) measurements reveal a high-field temperature minimum near the transverse-field Ising critical field for Hparallel[110] that persists and shifts only weakly upon field rotation. Tensor-network calculations show that the rotation-induced staggered transverse field rapidly lowers the Ising critical field and that the magnetic Grüneisen ratio changes sign near the high-field temperature minimum, consistent with experiment. Our results establish that a dominant MCE response can persist away from the Ising critical region, suggesting a route to magnetic cooling by tailoring anisotropic Zeeman-coupling configurations in quantum magnets.

    💡 亮点：本文研究了准一维 Ising 型反铁磁体 BaCo₂V₂O₈ 在横向磁场下的热力学性质，其中倾斜螺旋链结构和各向异性朗德 g 张量产生空间调制的 Zeeman 耦合。角分辨磁热效应测量发现，沿 [110] 方向施加磁场时，在横向场 Ising 临界场附近存在高场温度极小值，且旋转磁场方向时该极小值仍保持并仅发生弱移动。张量网络计算表明，旋转诱导的交错横向场会快速降低 Ising 临界场，磁 Grüneisen 比在高场温度极小值附近变号，这与实验结果一致。

    改进 Kane--Mele--Hubbard 模型中的交错磁异常霍尔效应与自旋--边缘锁定的手性模#
    Altermagnetic Anomalous Hall Effect and Spin--Edge-Locked Chiral Modes in a Modified Kane--Mele--Hubbard Model#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Mohsen Hafez-Torbati, Alireza Qaiumzadeh
    相关度
    7.5

    📄 摘要：我们在改进的 Kane--Mele--Hubbard 模型中建立了一条由关联驱动的交错磁异常霍尔效应（AHE）及其相关自旋--边缘锁定边缘态的实现路径。利用动力学平均场理论（DMFT），我们表明，在半填充条件下，增大 Hubbard 相互作用会使体系从具有反手性边缘态的金属顺磁相转变为面内 Néel 型反铁磁绝缘相；在该相中，残余反幺正对称性会禁止 AHE。空穴掺杂会诱导自旋翻转转变，使体系进入面外 Néel 型反铁磁相，从而破坏这一对称性并产生有限的异常霍尔电导，而且该电导可以延伸存在于强关联区域。不同于铁磁体中传统的自旋极化霍尔响应，交错磁 AHE 由两个对称相关的自旋扇区等量且相加地贡献，并伴随自旋--边缘锁定的手性态。我们的结果表明，载流子掺杂与自旋旋转不变的 Hubbard 相互作用足以实现交错磁 AHE，无需引入显式的 Ising 型相互作用，并为在关联过渡金属二硫族化物单层中实现该效应提供了一条现实的微观路径。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.24408v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We establish a correlation-driven route to the altermagnetic anomalous Hall effect (AHE) and its associated emphspin--edge-locked edge states in a modified Kane--Mele--Hubbard model. Using dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT), we show that, at half-filling, increasing the Hubbard interaction drives the system from a metallic paramagnetic phase hosting antichiral edge states into an insulating in-plane Néel-type antiferromagnetic phase, in which a residual antiunitary symmetry forbids the AHE. Hole doping induces a spin-flop transition to an out-of-plane Néel-type antiferromagnetic phase, thereby breaking this symmetry and generating a finite anomalous Hall conductivity that persists into the strongly correlated regime. Distinct from a conventional spin-polarized Hall response in ferromagnets, the altermagnetic AHE receives equal and additive contributions from the two symmetry-related spin sectors and is accompanied by spin--edge-locked chiral states. Our results demonstrate that carrier doping and spin-rotationally invariant Hubbard interactions are sufficient to realize the altermagnetic AHE, without invoking an explicitly Ising-like interaction, and provide a realistic microscopic route toward its realization in correlated transition metal dichalcogenides monolayers.

    💡 亮点：本文在改进的 Kane--Mele--Hubbard 模型中，揭示了由电子关联驱动的交错磁异常霍尔效应及其自旋--边缘锁定边缘态。动力学平均场理论表明，空穴掺杂可诱导自旋翻转，使体系进入面外 Néel 型反铁磁相并产生有限异常霍尔电导。该效应由两个对称相关自旋扇区等量且相加地贡献，并伴随自旋--边缘锁定的手性态，无需显式 Ising 型相互作用即可实现。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    文献研究：EMRB: A Multi-Level Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Reasoning over Raw Electromagnetic Signals#
    EMRB: A Multi-Level Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Reasoning over Raw Electromagnetic Signals#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cs.AI👤 Mingxu Zhang, Ying Sun, Yuhan Li, Yang Ji, Dazhong Shen, Ke Zhang, Shan Huang
    相关度
    7
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.24086v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as code agents for scientific and engineering analysis, but their ability to analyze raw physical-layer measurements remains untested. We introduce EMRB (Electromagnetic Reasoning Benchmark), which evaluates whether LLMs can analyze raw I/Q data by writing and running code. EMRB contains 200 problems across five difficulty levels and 27 question types, from signal detection to OFDM design, generated from 11 signal types with verified ground truth. Unlike benchmarks built on preprocessed features or structured tables, EMRB provides only the raw capture; the quantities each question refers to must first be discovered through code. We evaluate 14 LLMs spanning proprietary, open-weight, and reasoning-oriented families. Scores range from 24.1% to 78.9%, with the mean dropping from 84.9% on basic measurement to 21.2% on system design. We also propose ReconPilot, a structured method that separates signal reconnaissance, targeted analysis, and self-verification. Across three backbones, ReconPilot raises the overall score by 3.8 to 17.6 points and improves 13 of 15 backbone-level combinations tested. All data and code are publicly released in hrefhttps://github.com/mingxuZhang2/EMRBtextcolorblueour GitHub repository.

    💡 亮点：arXiv:2608.24086v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as code agents for scientific and engineering analysis, but their ability to analyze raw physical-lay…

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”补充训练/验证集，再比较《EMRB: A Multi-Level Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Reasoning over Raw Electromagnetic Signals》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

    相互作用团簇伊辛链中的临界分叉与退禁闭量子临界性#
    Critical bifurcation and deconfined quantum criticality in an interacting cluster Ising chain#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Sourabh, Bachana Beradze, Mikheil Tsitsishvili, Alexander Nersesyan, Titas Chanda
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：我们利用张量网络方法和弱耦合场论研究了具有额外最近邻相互作用的团簇伊辛链。在不存在该相互作用时，Jordan-Wigner变换将模型分解为三个相同的马约拉纳链，它们由精确的O(3)味对称性联系起来。它们的共同质量在中心荷c=3/2的SU(2)2 Wess-Zumino-Novikov-Witten临界点处消失，该临界点将对称性保护拓扑团簇相与铁磁体分隔开来。该相互作用将O(3)约化为其循环子群C3，并将三重态分裂为单态和二重态；二者的能隙分别闭合，从而分叉形成伊辛(c=1/2)和高斯(c=1)临界线。对于排斥相互作用，第二个铁磁相在这两条临界线之间出现；对于吸引相互作用，则出现一个无序相。随后，高斯线分隔两个在朗道理论框架下不相容的铁磁体，因而实现了一条具有涌现O(2)对称性的退禁闭量子临界线；在该线上，我们独立提取的指数连续变化，但满足八顶点弱普适类中无参数关系β=(2ν-1)/4。在更强的排斥作用下，该临界线扩展为一个具有非公度代数关联的延展无能隙浮动相，并通过Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless相变进入该相。所有这些相及其之间的相变均可由弱耦合理论捕捉。在超出该理论适用范围后，我们的模拟揭示出一个破坏平移对称性的反铁磁相，该相通过一级相变到达。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.24248v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We investigate the cluster Ising chain with an additional nearest-neighbor interaction using tensor-network methods and a weak-coupling field theory. Without the interaction, the Jordan-Wigner transformation decomposes the model into a triplet of identical Majorana chains related by an exact O(3) flavor symmetry. Their common mass vanishes at the SU(2)₂ Wess-Zumino-Novikov-Witten critical point with central charge c = 3/2 separating the symmetry-protected topological cluster phase from a ferromagnet. The interaction reduces O(3) to its cyclic subgroup C₃, splitting the triplet into a singlet and a doublet whose gaps close separately, producing a critical bifurcation into Ising (c = 1/2) and Gaussian (c = 1) critical lines. A second ferromagnetic phase opens between these critical lines for repulsive interactions and a disordered phase for attractive ones. The Gaussian line then separates two Landau-incompatible ferromagnets, realizing a deconfined quantum critical line with emergent O(2) symmetry, along which our independently extracted exponents vary continuously yet satisfy the parameter-free relation β = (2ν - 1)/4 of the eight-vertex weak universality class. At stronger repulsion this line opens into an extended gapless floating phase with incommensurate algebraic correlations, entered through Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transitions. All of these phases and the transitions between them are captured by the weak-coupling theory. Beyond its regime of validity, our simulations reveal a translation-symmetry-breaking antiferromagnet reached through first-order transitions.

    💡 亮点：该工作研究含最近邻相互作用的团簇伊辛链，发现相互作用将原有的SU(2)2临界点分裂为伊辛和高斯临界线。高斯临界线分隔两个朗道理论不相容的铁磁相，并实现具有涌现O(2)对称性的退禁闭量子临界线。较强排斥作用下，该临界线扩展为具有非公度代数关联的无能隙浮动相。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”补充训练/验证集，再比较《相互作用团簇伊辛链中的临界分叉与退禁闭量子临界性》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

    紧凑型无损微秒级超快脉冲磁场发生器#
    Compact nondestructive generator for microsecond-class ultrafast pulsed magnetic fields#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Y. Ishii, A. Ikeda, H. Sun, H. Oike, Y. H. Matsuda
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：脉冲磁场的扫描速率是探索物质中非平衡现象的关键因素。然而，由于传统脉冲磁场发生器的大电感和电容所伴随的较大时间常数，以及功率器件和系统本身的残余电感，无损产生超快脉冲磁场具有挑战性。我们开发了一种采用商用先进超快功率器件的新型紧凑脉冲磁场发生器，能够产生最高约 1 T、脉冲持续时间为 3.8 微秒的超快脉冲磁场。我们展示了新开发系统的性能，并将实验获得的磁场波形与简单电路模拟的结果进行了比较。此外，我们还利用该系统演示了对钴粉末的磁化测量以及对 CdS 单晶的法拉第旋转测量。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.23584v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: The sweep rate of pulsed magnetic fields is a key factor in exploring of nonequilibrium phenomena in matter. However, the nondestructive generation of ultrafast pulsed magnetic fields is challenging because of the large time constants associated with the large inductance and capacitance of conventional pulsed magnetic field generators, as well as the residual inductance of the power devices and the system itself. We have developed a new compact pulsed magnetic field generator using commercially available state-of-the-art ultrafast power devices, enabling the generation of ultrafast pulsed magnetic fields up to approximately 1 T with a pulse duration of 3.8 μs. We present the performance of the newly developed system and compare the experimentally obtained magnetic field waveforms with the results of a simple circuit simulation. In addition, we demonstrate magnetization measurements on cobalt powder and Faraday rotation measurements on CdS single crystal using the developed system.

    💡 亮点：脉冲磁场的扫描速率是探索物质非平衡现象的关键，但传统设备受大电感、电容及残余电感限制，难以无损产生超快脉冲磁场。本文开发了一种采用商用先进超快功率器件的紧凑型发生器，可产生约 1 T、持续时间为 3.8 微秒的脉冲磁场。作者还展示了该装置在钴粉磁化测量和 CdS 单晶法拉第旋转测量中的应用。

    考虑轨道磁矩的I型多重外尔半金属磁光输运#
    Magneto-optical transport in type-I multi-Weyl semimetals in the presence of orbital magnetic moment#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Panchlal Prabhat, Amit Gupta
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：多重外尔半金属是具有较高单极荷的外尔节点并呈现强各向异性色散的拓扑量子材料。我们在纳入贝里曲率（BC）和轨道磁矩（OMM）修正的半经典玻尔兹曼框架下，研究了具有任意单极荷J的倾斜I型多重外尔半金属的线性和非线性磁光输运。我们推导了外部电场和磁场存在时纵向、霍尔、平面霍尔以及二次谐波磁电导的解析表达式。所得电导分量表现出随化学势和单极荷变化的特征标度关系，反映了多重外尔节点的各向异性色散。我们进一步阐明了贝里曲率和轨道磁矩贡献之间的相互作用，并表明轨道磁矩通常会抑制由贝里曲率诱导的磁电导；然而，在特定输运几何中，轨道磁矩的贡献可能与贝里曲率贡献相当，甚至超过后者。我们的结果为实验探测锥体倾斜、高阶单极荷、贝里曲率和轨道磁矩共同作用对多重外尔半金属磁光响应的影响提供了可观测的特征。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2406.09888v3 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Multi-Weyl semimetals (mWSMs) are topological quantum materials whose Weyl nodes carry higher monopole charges and exhibit strongly anisotropic dispersions. We investigate the linear and nonlinear magneto-optical transport of tilted type-I mWSMs with arbitrary monopole charge J within a semiclassical Boltzmann framework that incorporates both Berry-curvature (BC) and orbital-magnetic-moment (OMM) corrections. We derive analytical expressions for the longitudinal, Hall, planar Hall and second-harmonic magnetoconductivities in the presence of external electric and magnetic fields. The resulting conductivity components exhibit characteristic scaling with the chemical potential and monopole charge, reflecting the anisotropic dispersion of multi-Weyl nodes. We further elucidate the interplay between the BC and OMM contributions and show that the OMM generally suppresses the BC-induced magnetoconductivity, while its contribution can become comparable to or larger than the BC contribution for specific transport geometries. Our results provide experimentally accessible signatures of the combined effects of cone tilt, higher monopole charge, Berry curvature, and orbital magnetic moment in the magneto-optical response of multi-Weyl semimetals.

    💡 亮点：本文在包含贝里曲率和轨道磁矩修正的半经典玻尔兹曼框架下，研究具有任意单极荷J的倾斜I型多重外尔半金属的线性与非线性磁光输运。作者推导了纵向、霍尔、平面霍尔及二次谐波磁电导的解析表达式，并分析其随化学势和单极荷的特征标度关系。结果显示，轨道磁矩通常会抑制贝里曲率诱导的磁电导，但在特定输运几何中，其贡献可能与贝里曲率贡献相当甚至更大。

    颗粒铝在锗中诱导超导：面向空穴自旋混合器件#
    Granular aluminum induced superconductivity in germanium for hole spin-based hybrid devices#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Giorgio Fabris, Paul Falthansl-Scheinecker, Devashish Shah, Daniel Michel Pino, Maksim Borovkov, Anton Bubis, Kevin Roux, Dina Sokolova, Alejandro Andres Juanes, Tommaso Costanzo, Inas Taha, Aziz Genç, Jordi Arbiol, Stefano Calcaterra, Afonso De Cerdeira Oliveira, Daniel Chrastina, Giovanni Isella, Ruben Seoane Souto, Maria Jose Calderon, Ramon Aguado, Jose Carlos Abadillo-Uriel, Georgios Katsaros
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：在超导体-半导体混合结构中，超导性和自旋极化是相互竞争的效应，因为磁场会破坏库珀对。利用薄膜和面内磁场可以将二者结合起来；这种方法推动了对马约拉纳零模、Kitaev链和Andreev自旋量子比特（ASQ）的研究，但对于面内g因子较小的材料，或需要面外磁场的情形，仍然具有挑战性。我们证明，颗粒铝（grAl）可以克服这一限制；颗粒铝由嵌入非晶氧化物基体中的纳米米级铝颗粒组成。通过在Ge/SiGe异质结构上沉积颗粒铝，我们诱导出具有305微电子伏特BCS峰的硬超导隙，并使其在面内和面外方向都具有抗磁场能力，从而允许Yu-Shiba-Rusinov（YSR）态的塞曼劈裂超过50微电子伏特（12 GHz）。利用这种鲁棒性，我们揭示了空穴物理的特征，并讨论了在平面锗中驱动Andreev自旋量子比特的一种机制，而且该机制不受Rashba自旋轨道耦合强度的限制。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2602.21364v4 Announce Type: replace Abstract: In superconductor-semiconductor hybrid structures, superconductivity and spin polarization are competing effects as magnetic fields break Cooper pairs. They can be combined using thin films and in-plane magnetic fields, an approach that enabled the pursuit of Majorana zero modes, Kitaev chains, and Andreev spin qubits (ASQs), but remains challenging for materials with small in-plane g-factors or when out-of-plane fields are required. We demonstrate that granular aluminium (grAl), composed of nanometre-scale aluminium grains embedded in an amorphous oxide matrix, can overcome this limitation. By depositing grAl on Ge/SiGe heterostructures, we induce a hard superconducting gap with BCS peaks at 305~μeV and magnetic-field resilience for both the in-plane and out-of-plane directions, allowing Zeeman splitting of Yu-Shiba-Rusinov (YSR) states beyond 50~μeV (12 GHz). Leveraging this robustness, we reveal signatures of hole physics and discuss a driving mechanism for ASQs regardless of the strength of the Rashba spin-orbit coupling in planar germanium.

    💡 亮点：在超导体-半导体混合结构中，磁场会破坏库珀对，使超导性与自旋极化相互竞争。本文表明，由纳米尺度铝颗粒嵌入非晶氧化物基体构成的颗粒铝，可在Ge/SiGe异质结构中诱导具有305微电子伏特BCS峰的硬超导隙，并同时耐受面内和面外磁场。借助这种鲁棒性，作者观察到超过50微电子伏特（12 GHz）的Yu-Shiba-Rusinov态塞曼劈裂，并讨论了在平面锗中不依赖Rashba自旋轨道耦合强度驱动Andreev自旋量子比特的机制。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    文献研究：MnemoDyn: Learning Resting State Dynamics from 40K FMRI sequences#
    MnemoDyn: Learning Resting State Dynamics from 40K FMRI sequences#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Sourav Pal, Viet Luong, Hoseok Lee, Tingting Dan, Guorong Wu, Richard Davidson, Won Hwa Kim, Vikas Singh
    相关度
    5.5
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.23936v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We present a dynamical-systems based model for resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (rs-fMRI), trained on a dataset of roughly 40K rs-fMRI sequences covering a wide variety of public and available-by-permission datasets. While most existing proposals use transformer backbones, we utilize multi-resolution temporal modeling of the dynamics across parcellated brain regions. We show that MnemoDyn is compute efficient and generalizes very well across diverse populations and scanning protocols. When benchmarked against current state-of-the-art transformer-based approaches, MnemoDyn consistently delivers superior reconstruction quality. Overall, we find that with such large-scale pre-training on (non-proprietary) rs-fMRI datasets, we get a highly performant model for various downstream tasks. Our results also provide evidence of the efficacy of the model on small sample size studies which has implications for neuroimaging studies at large where resting state fMRI is a commonly acquired imaging modality.
